# The Catacombs - Floor VII
Aliases: F7, Floor 7, Floor VII, M7, Master Mode - Floor VII, Master Mode Floor 7, Master Mode Floor VII, The Catacombs - Floor 7, Wither Dragons, Withered Dragon, Withered Dragons
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeon Floors, More info needed, Catacombs, Dungeons
[The Catacombs - Floor VII]
Type: Dungeon Floor
Dungeon: Catacombs
Boss: [[Maxor]]; [[Storm]]; [[Goldor]]; [[Necron]]
Status: The Wither Lords
Dungeon Size: Large (Map Size 6x6)
Party Size: 1-5
Requirements to enter: Catacombs XXIV (24) ([[Catacombs]])
Base XP: 30k
The Catacombs - Floor VII is the seventh and final floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], featuring FINAL-BOSS [[Maxor]], [[Storm]], [[Goldor]], and [[Necron]] as the bosses. [[The Wither King]] appears in a 5th phase during Master Mode.
## Overview
Floor VII introduces several new mechanics.
- Several mobs are exclusive to Floor VII: the [[Helmets|Zombie Lord]], [[Skeleton Lord]], [[Wither Miner]], [[Wither Guard]], and [[Necron|Necron's Apostle]].
- A [[Skeletor Prime]] has a 100% chance to spawn in place of a [[Skeletor]].
- The [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Ice Fill]] puzzle is exclusive to this floor.
New mobs that appear include:
- [[Zombie Lord]]
- [[Skeleton Lord]]
New obtainable items include:
- [[Wither Armor]]
- [[Necron's Handle]]
- [[Wither Catalyst]]
- [[Precursor Gear]]
- [[Wither Blood]]
- [[Wither Cloak Sword]]
- [[Auto Recombobulator]]
- [[Diamante's Handle]]
- [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]]
- [[Jolly Pink Rock]]
- [[Bigfoot's Bola]]
- [[Soul Eater]]
- [[One For All]]
- [[Zombie Lord Armor]]
- [[Skeleton Lord Armor]]
- [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Implosion]], [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]], and [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Shadow Warp]]
- [[Dark Claymore]] (M7 exclusive)
New craftable items include:
- [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]]
- [[Hyperion]]
- [[Valkyrie]]
- [[Astraea]]
- [[Scylla]]
- [[Necron's Armor]]
- [[Storm's Armor]]
- [[Goldor's Armor]]
- [[Maxor's Armor]]
- [[Catacombs Expert Ring]]
- [[Wither Relic]]
## Boss Fight
NOTE: Most teleportation abilities and movement abilities are disabled during the fight, including:
- [[Aspect of the End]]
- [[Aspect of the Void]]
- [[Etherwarp Conduit]]
- [[Sinseeker Scythe]]
- [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]]
- [[Grappling Hook]]
- [[Leaping Sword]]
- [[Silk-Edge Sword]]
### Phase 1: Maxor
Time for me to blast you away for good!
— [[Maxor]]
Maxor's arena: players must collect Energy Crystals and lure Maxor to a beam to damage him.
[[Maxor]]'s phase takes place in a factory. Players must collect Energy Crystals, located at the top of the arena, by completing a series of parkour and place them at platforms located near the bottom of the arena. Once this is completed, Maxor must be lured to the end of the arena to be damaged by a beam. Once Maxor dies, the party progresses to the next phase.
Maxor has the following attacks:
Name: Description
Wither Skulls: Maxor will rapidly shoot 3 Wither Skulls at the target, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Rapid Fire Skulls: Maxor will stop moving and begin rapidly firing Wither Skulls, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Detonate: Maxor will spawn and detonate a block of TNT, exploding after several seconds, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Maxor's Rage: Upon being damaged by the beam, Maxor will become enraged and begin summoning frequent explosions around him.
A [[Bonzo's Staff]], [[Jerry-chine Gun]] or [[Spring Boots]] can be used for mobility during this phase to get the Energy Crystals.
### Phase 2: Storm
The Age of Men is over, we are creating tens, hundreds of withers!
— [[Storm]]
Storm's arena: players must lure Storm under a pillar and crush him using the corresponding pressure pads.
[[Storm]]'s phase takes place in a large arena with a main platform in the middle and four platforms in the corners, connected by thin bridges. Four pillars are found in the main platform, which are connected to four corresponding pressure pads in the corners: players must lure Storm underneath a pillar while another player activates its corresponding pressure pad, crushing Storm in the process.
[[Storm]] must use Giga Lightning at least once before being able to be crushed.
If the party fails to kill Storm and no functioning pillars are left, the run will be softlocked and the party will have to restart.
Storm has the following attacks:
Name: Description
Wither Skulls: Storm will rapidly shoot 3 Wither Skulls at the target, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Lightning Fireball: Storm will shoot fireballs at nearby players, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Static Field: Storm will summon lots of lightning around the arena, dealing large [[Damage]] to all players who are hit.
Giga Lightning: Storm will fly to the center of the arena and stop moving, charging up a Giga Lightning for 10s, before summoning lightning throughout the entire arena, instantly killing everyone who is not standing under a functional pillar: this can be bypassed with a [[Wither Cloak Sword]], [[Mithril Coat]], or [[Gyrokinetic Wand]].; During Master Mode, the charge time is reduced to 5s.
Storm's Rage: Upon being crushed by the pillar, Storm will become enraged and begin summoning frequent explosions around him.
### Phase 3: Goldor
I am the death zone, you are smart to flee.
— [[Goldor]]
  See also: [[Terminal|Terminals]]
Goldor's arena: players must complete terminals and devices and flick levers to open the gate.
[[Goldor]]'s phase takes place in a factory composed of corridors in the outline of a square, concluding with a path extending into the Core Entrance. Players must both complete a series of parkour, solving [[Terminal|Terminals]] and devices at the same time, while slowing down Goldor as he slowly approaches.
Lava surrounds the bottom of the moat. Falling into the lava will launch the player upwards in one of 2 ways:
- Regular: You are launched into the air at a standard height ([More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) blocks).
- Super: Looking upwards at a pitch of -40 or lower triggers a Super lava bounce, boosting you 30% higher than a Regular jump.
The Core Entrance, where Goldor can be damaged.
Once all Terminals and devices are completed and all levers and activated, the gate will be destroyed and the party can progress: the final section leads into the Core Entrance, where Goldor will close in on the party. During this period, Goldor can be damaged: successfully killing Goldor in time will allow the party to progress to the fourth phase.
Goldor has the following attacks:
Name: Description
Greatsword: Goldor will launch a gold greatsword in a player's direction, dealing immense [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]] on impact.
Detonate: Goldor will detonate sets of TNT located near the ceiling, igniting them and dealing immense [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]] to nearby players.
Instakill: All players who are behind Goldor's path of movement or went through the gate before a section is complete will be instantly killed.
Goldor's Rage: Upon completion of all Terminals and subsequent entrance to the core, Goldor will close in and fire a barrage of wither skulls at multiple players and get a damaging aura, dealing immense [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
### Phase 4: Necron
Sometimes when you have a problem, you just need to destroy it all and start again.
— [[Necron]]
Necron's arena: players must damage him continually while avoiding his attacks.
[[Necron]]'s phase takes place in a circular arena with a platform in the middle, surrounded by a lava moat. Necron will use his abilities against the players: after taking enough damage, he gives the players blindness for a short time and teleports to the middle platform.
Unlike the other bosses, Necron will always be able to be damaged traditionally. Once Necron is defeated, the run is completed and players can claim loot: during Master Mode, killing Necron will allow players to progress to the fifth phase.
Necron has the following abilities:
Name: Description
Wither Skulls: Necron will rapidly shoot 3 Wither Skulls at the target, dealing more [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]] than [[Maxor]] or [[Storm]].
Empowered Greatsword: Necron will summon 4 diamond greatswords, similar to [[Goldor]], rotating around Necron and flying towards a random player, dealing immense [[Damage]] on impact.
Lightning Fireball: Necron will shoot fireballs that strike lightning at nearby players, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
Static Field: Necron will summon lightning around the arena, dealing large [[Damage]] to all players who are hit.
Laser Wall: Necron will cast 64 Laser beams that rotate clockwise. Contact with those laser beams deal 25% of their [[Health]] as [[True Damage]], akin to Broken Heart Radiation used by the Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
Nuclear Frenzy: Necron will teleport to the center platform and begins exploding rapidly every second: taking damage reduces the size of the explosion, and not taking damage will increase it. After a certain threshold, the Nuclear Frenzy will end stunning him for a amount of time.
Necron's Rage: Necron will stay still in the air and fire a dozen Wither Skulls at a nearby player, dealing large [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].
### Phase 5: The Wither King
Mortals fighting against supreme beings, what a moving spectacle.
— [[The Wither King]]
Kaeman's arena: players must kill each Withered Dragon near its corresponding statue.
[[The Wither King]]'s phase, exclusive to Master Mode - Floor VII, takes place in a giant arena surrounded by giant statues of dragons. Players must collect Corrupted Relics around the arena and place them, summoning the Wither King and two Withered Dragons. [[Apostle|Apostles]] will also spawn.
There are 5 Withered Dragons, each of which must be killed while near their corresponding statue to be successfully completed. A list of Withered Dragons are found below:
Name: Behavior
[[Apex Dragon]]: Apex will swoop down and attack nearby players, dealing immense [[Damage]] and knockback on impact.
[[Soul Dragon]]: Soul will teleport to random locations throughout the arena, and will teleport to a random statue (including its own) every 3 times it teleports.
[[Flame Dragon]]: Flame will shoot players with fireballs and occasionally swoop down, leaving behind a trail of fire as it flies.
[[Ice Dragon]]: Ice will occasionally swoop down, leaving behind a trail of ice that heavily restricts movement and trapping players in cubes of ice.
[[Power Dragon]]: Power will occasionally fly towards the center of the arena and perform a Mega TNT attack, which can be prevented if enough damage is dealt: a successful Mega TNT will deal immense [[True Damage]] to anyone in the center of the arena.
The Wither King also has some attacks of his own:
Name: Behavior
Reel In: The Wither King will pull players who get too close to it using its tentacles and instantly kill them.
Atomizing Ray: The Wither King will shoot a giant beam from its head and look throughout the arena, dealing colossal damage to players standing within.
Dimensional Slash: The Wither King will randomly strike players with lightning.
Once all dragons are alive at the same time, a hand reaches up from the center of the arena, grabbing all players in the party and killing them instantly, resulting in a fail. Once all dragons are dead, the run will be completed.
## Loot
The Catacombs - Floor VII's [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] function slightly differently: players will unlock higher-tiered chests as they progress. Reaching the boss fight unlocks the Wood chest, beating [[Maxor]] unlocks the Gold chest, beating [[Storm]] unlocks the Diamond chest, beating [[Goldor]] unlocks the Emerald Chest, beating [[Necron]] unlocks the Obsidian chest, and beating [[The Wither King]] automatically unlocks the Bedrock chest.
[[Wither Armor]] pieces and [[Necron's Handle|Necron's Handles]] obtained from Master Mode - Floor VII have a small chance to get a cosmetic Shiny prefix. The item will also have an enchantment glint even if it is not enchanted.

### Music Discs
There is a 2% chance for the [[Essence]] in an Obsidian or Bedrock Dungeon Reward Chest to be replaced with one of these [[Music Discs]]:
- [[Dungeon Disc]]
- [[Clown Disc]]
- [[Necron Disc]]
- [[Watcher Disc]]
- [[Old Disc]] (Master Mode)
## Trivia
- When the floor first released, Goldor's phase was bugged and could not be completed.
- When Master Mode - Floor VII first released, Shiny [[Wither Armor]] had the same scaling mechanic as most Dungeon Mob drops, adding up to a +50% stat boost and upgrading its base rarity in the case of a perfect stat boost.
## History

|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|The Catacombs - Floor VII can be viewed in [[Mort]].|

# Vitality
Aliases: Vitality/Alpha
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Player Statistics
[Vitality]
Type: Stat
Increasing: Vitality ([[Attributes]]), [[Healing Ring]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E028
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Glistering Melon Slice ()
Vitality is one of the player's [[Stats|Combat Stats]]. It serves as a pool, similar to [[Mana]], that healing abilities draw from.
## Usage
Vitality is a stat that functions similar to [[Mana]]: it is a pool that items with healing abilities (e.g. [[Florid Zombie Sword]], [[Wand of Atonement]]) draw from and certain abilities and [[Enchantments]] grant (e.g. [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]], [[Drain]]).
After using any healing ability for the first time, a Vitality display appears on the [[Player]]'s action bar.
## Regenerating Vitality
Vitality regenerates at a rate of 5% of the player's maximum Vitality every second. It can also be regenerated by using certain effects:
|Slot|Item|Type||
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Drain]]|[[Drain]]|Weapon Enchantment|Regen +0.5-3 Vitality every time player hits a mob. (1s cooldown)|
|[[Last Stand]]|[[Last Stand]]|Armor Enchantment|Regen +2-10 Vitality when falling below 40% [[Health]]. (30s cooldown)|
|[[Turtle Pet]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Turtle Pet]]|Pet|Regen +5.05-10 Vitality after taking 10 hits.|
|[[Armor of Growth]]|[[Armor of Growth]]|Armor|Regen +5 Vitality when killing a mob. (5s cooldown)|
## Increasing Base Vitality
Base Vitality is 100. It can be increased in certain ways:
|Icon|Method|Bonus|
|-|-|-|
|[[Marigold]]|Unbreaking perk|1 per level up to 5 Vitality|
## Increasing Bonus Vitality
Bonus Vitality can be increased in certain ways:
### Pets
Certain [[Pets]] grant Vitality. They must be equipped to grant their effects.
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ghoul Pet]]|[[Ghoul Pet]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) - LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Combat Pet ([[Pets]])|+0.15-15|
### Pet Items
Certain [[Pet Items]] grant Vitality. The [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] must be equipped on the player's active [[Pets|Pet]] to take effect.
|Slot|Name|Rarity||
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minos Relic]]|[[Minos Relic]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Increases all pet stats by 33.3%|
|[[Hephaestus Relic]]|[[Hephaestus Relic]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Increases all pet stats by 50%|
### Weapons
Certain [[Weapons]] grant Vitality.
|Slot|Item|Rarity||

Zombie Commander Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Armor]] Set that can be obtained from [[Zombie Commander|Zombie Commanders]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]-[[The Catacombs - Floor VII|VII]]. It is an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set, therefore it is affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.
While in Dungeons, its stats will receive a percentage buff based on one's [[Dungeoneering]] skill level with additional 10% stat buffs dependent on the number of [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] it has. The stats listed are the armor being outside of Dungeons, as the dungeon buff given to the armor varies with the number of stars and the [[Catacombs]] skill the player has.

Zombie Knight Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Armor]] set that can be obtained from [[Zombie Knight|Zombie Knights]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] (3) and onwards. As a [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]], its [[Stats]] are randomized. It is an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set, therefore it is affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.
While in [[Dungeons]], its stats will receive a percentage buff based on one's [[Catacombs]] skill level with additional 10% stat buffs dependent on the number of [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] it has. The stats listed are the armor being outside of [[Dungeons]], as the dungeon buff given to the armor varies with the number of Stars and the [[Catacombs]] skill a player has.

## Trivia
This armor set is a good substitute for [[Berserk|Berserkers]] and [[Archer|Archers]], in case they cannot afford the chestplate of a [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin]] or [[Necron's Armor|Necron]] set. A Zombie Knight chestplate with maximized random stats is relatively cheap compared to those sets.

Zombie Lord Armor is an [[Armor|Armor Set]] in [[Dungeons]]. It is dropped from [[Zombie Lord|Zombie Lords]]. It can be used at [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXV (25)]]. It is an upgrade of [[Zombie Commander Armor]], having the improved quantities of the same stats of the latter. It is a moderately tanky set with high [[Health]] and [[Defense]], and is geared more for the [[Healer]] and the [[Tank]] classes. However its lack of much [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] make it less viable for damage-centered classes, such as [[Berserk|Berserker]] or [[Archer]]. It is an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set, therefore it is affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.

## Tips
- Zombie Lord Armor is quite common from [[Zombie Lord|Zombie Lords]], and can be used as a budget alternative to the more expensive Healer and Tank sets like [[Necromancer Lord Armor]], because it sells for below 200,000 [[Coins]] a piece on the [[Auction House]].
  - However because of the sizable [[Effective Health|EHP]] difference between [[Necromancer Lord Armor]] and the Zombie Lord set, it is recommended to save up the [[Coins]] to get pieces of the Necromancer Lord set.
- A player found through testing that the top quality Zombie Lord Chestplate gives the same amount of [[Effective Health|EHP]] as the [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin Chestplate]], however giving far less damage stat buffs.

Zombie Soldier Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons]] [[Armor]] Set that can be obtained from [[Zombie Soldier|Zombie Soldiers]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and above. Its Full Set Bonus gives the player 30 [[Defense]] for every set of Zombie Soldier Armor within 30 blocks. It is an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set, therefore it is affected by the [[Zombie Pet]]'s Living Dead perk.
While in Dungeons, its stats will receive a percentage buff based on one's [[Dungeoneering]] skill level with additional 10% stat buffs dependent on the number of [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] it has. The stats listed are the armor being outside of [[Dungeons]], as the dungeon buff given to the armor varies with the number of stars and the [[Catacombs]] skill the player has.

## Trivia
- This armor set is a good budget dungeon armor set when paired with a [[Bonzo's Mask]].
- For lower-leveled dungeon players, this set is one of the best sets in dungeons, dealing less damage compared to [[Adaptive Armor]], and costing only about 5% as much as full [[Adaptive Armor]]. Additionally, it gives more [[Effective Health|EHP]] than Adaptive and it is on par with [[Zombie Knight Armor]] for [[Tank|Tanks]].
- The helmet should be replaced with [[Bonzo's Mask]] for players new to [[Dungeons]]. For [[Mage|Mages]], both the [[Bonzo's Mask]] and the [[Dark Goggles]]/ [[Shadow Goggles]] are good options, though the latter option rewards high damage at the cost of low effective health. If one choose the Goggles, one is advised to add [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] to the Goggles to compensate for the lack of base [[Health]] and [[Defense]].

# Account & Profile Upgrades
Aliases: Account Upgrade, Account Upgrades, Account and Profile Upgrades, Account/Profile Upgrades, Island Size, Profile Upgrade, Profile Upgrades
Categories: Templates with deprecated argument names, Community Center, Upgrades
Account & Profile Upgrades, accessible from [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]] allow players to upgrade their account and profile in various ways. Once unlocked, these upgrades are permanent. These upgrades are often free in the early levels, requiring nothing but time to upgrade, but may cost [[SkyBlock Gems]] in the later levels. These upgrades can also be skipped, allowing players access to them immediately, at a default cost of 144 [[SkyBlock Gems]] per real life day skipped (6 [[SkyBlock Gems]] per hour), with some upgrades offering cheaper skip costs.
## Overview
Here is a quick overview of which upgrades are available for free, and which are placed behind a paywall.
In order to max out every upgrade, 17,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] are required (~$123 if buying most expensive pack). Solo players who opt to skip the co-op slots upgrade only require 15,550 [[SkyBlock Gems]] (~$110).
|Upgrade Type|Upgrade|Obtainable for Free|Behind the Pay-Wall|Cost to max out (without skipping time)|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Profile|Island Size|169x169|241x241|1,675 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Profile|Minion Slots|+5 slots|||
|Profile|Guests Limit|+1 slot|+4 slots (+5 total)|200 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Profile|Co-op Slots||+3 slots|1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Profile|Coins Allowance|+50k [[Coins]]|||
|Account|Armor Wardrobe Slots|+1 slot|+8 slots (+9 total)|3,750 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Equipment Wardrobe Slots||+9 slots|2,250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Loadout Slots||+9 slots|4,500 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Heart of the Mountain||+3 slots|450 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Heart of the Forest||+3 slots|450 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Ender Chest Pages|+1 page|+7 pages (+8 total)|975 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Accessory Bag|+12 slots|||
|Account|Sack of Sacks|+4 slots|+12 slots (+16 total)|1,200 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Magic Find|+5 [[Magic Find]]|||
|Account|Bazaar Flipper|+7 orders, -0.125% tax|+7 orders, -0.125% tax; (+14 orders, -0.25% tax total)|100 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|Account|Garden Fortune|+40 [[Farming Fortune]] while on [[The Garden]]|||
Players who spend [[SkyBlock Gems]] on all possible upgrades will get: 3 extra slots for their [[Co-op]], 4 extra simultaneous [[Guesting|Guests]] to their island, 72 additional blocks of width to their [[Private Island]] (this almost doubles the square footage), 7 additional pages to their [[Ender Chest]], 12 additional slots for their [[Sack of Sacks]], 0.125% reduced tax in the Bazaar, and 7 additional simultaneous orders in the [[Bazaar]], 8 [[Wardrobe|Armor Wardrobe]], 9 [[Equipment Wardrobe]] and 9 [[Loadouts|Loadout]] slots, 3 [[Heart of the Mountain]] and 3 [[Heart of the Forest]] slots.
## Profile Upgrades
Profile Upgrades upgrade the profile they are accessed on, and are shared across co-op members.

|-|-|-|-|-|-|

# Arrows
Aliases: Armorshred Arrow, Arrow, Bouncy Arrow, Emerald-tipped Arrow, Explosive Arrow, Glue Arrow, Gold-tipped Arrow, Icy Arrow, Magma Arrow, Nansorb Arrow, Redstone-tipped Arrow, Reinforced Iron Arrow
Categories: Templates with deprecated argument names, Bows, Arrows
NOTE
In order to use arrows from your [[Quiver]], make sure you have the [[Arrow Swapper]] set to that arrow!
Arrows are consumable projectiles that are used by [[Bows]] and [[Shortbows]].
## Usage
Arrows can be used when held in the inventory or [[Quiver]]. Upon firing any [[Bows|Bow]], 1 arrow is consumed: bows cannot be used if the player lacks any arrows in their inventory or Quiver. Bows that fire multiple arrows at once, such as the [[Terminator]], only consume 1 arrow per shot.
Several methods to reduce the number of arrows that are consumed exist:
- [[Infinite Quiver]] increases the chance that an arrow will not be consumed upon firing, up to 30% at [[Infinite Quiver|Infinite Quiver X]].
- The [[Skeleton Master Armor|Skeleton Master Chestplate]] prevents arrows from being consumed.
Base [[Damage]] from arrows are added to the base [[Damage]] of the bow that the player is currently using.
Additionally, multiple types of arrows exist: special arrows can be purchased from [[Jax]] in the [[Archery Range]], requiring higher levels of Target Practice completed to purchase better arrows. These cannot be removed from the Quiver. [[Jax]] also sells the [[Arrow Swapper]], which allows the player to swap the type of arrows that are consumed in the Quiver.

## Trivia
- When holding a non-[[Shortbows|Shortbow]], [[SkyBlock Menu]] in the player's hotbar will turn into "Quiver Arrows". When an arrow is shot, the Quiver Arrows will subtract one (64 quiver arrows into 63 quiver arrows), then regenerate back.
- When using Explosive Arrows and spamming Multi-Shot Bows or Shortbows to hit the ground, the arrows can do many hits rapidly. This is used do destroy [[Voidgloom Seraph|Voidgloom Seraphim]]'s Hitshield as fast as possible, especially with a [[Terminator]], which is both a Shortbow and a Triple-Shot Bow.
- Before being changed, the explosion from your own Explosive Arrows could damage and even kill you. The message read "You were killed by [player name]" (or "[player name] was killed by [player name]" for others).
- The Nansorb Arrow is be named after Pierce Brosnan, as Nansorb is Brosnan backwards and the arrow pierces through mobs.

# Mithril Gourmand
Aliases: Gourmand
Categories: Templates with deprecated argument names, Dwarven Mines
[Mithril Gourmand]
The Mithril Gourmand is an active event that can happen in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Mechanics
During the event, certain area in the [[Dwarven Mines]] will be selected, and in there mining [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]] will have a 30% chance to drop a [[Tasty Mithril]]. 100 (1 stack plus 36)–200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Tasty Mithril]] has to be given to [[Don Expresso]] to complete the event.
Mithril Gourmand event lasts for 10 minutes. If requirement is fulfilled, timer will be shortened to 1 minute.
### Rewards
If the player gives [[Don Expresso]] at least 10 [[Tasty Mithril]], they will gain extra rewards. Similarly to the [[Raffle]] event, players need to bring the items to a specific location, this location can be reached with the command /tptodonexpresso. Also similarly to [[Raffle]], collected Tasty Mithril is only useful in the event during which it has been obtained.
Rewards:
| 10 [[Tasty Mithril]] donated Donation milestone reached| 10 [[Tasty Mithril]] donated Donation milestone is not reached|Less than 10 [[Tasty Mithril]] donated Donation milestone reached|Less than 10 [[Tasty Mithril]] donated Donation milestone is not reached|
|-|-|-|-|
|+25,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])|+20,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])|+5,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])||
|220 HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) (only if HotM level is not maxed ([[Heart of the Mountain]]))|200 HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) (only if HotM level is not maxed ([[Heart of the Mountain]]))|||
|20,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 100 × [[Tasty Mithril]] Donated|9,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 50 × [[Tasty Mithril]] Donated|200 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 50 × [[Tasty Mithril]] Donated|50 × [[Tasty Mithril]] Donated ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]|
|40 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)|30 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)|20 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)||
|2 Mining Items|1 Mining Item|||
Mining Items have an equal chance to be any of the items below:
|Slot|Item|
|-|-|
|[[Mining Exp Boost]]|1x RARE Mining Exp Boost ([[Mining Exp Boost]])|
|[[Flowstate]]|1 [[Enchanted Book]] (Flowstate I ([[Flowstate]]))|
|[[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|1 [[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|
|[[Dwarven Treasure]]|1 [[Dwarven Treasure]]|
|[[Titanium Minecart]]|1 [[Titanium Minecart]]|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event announced|⚑ The Mithril Gourmand event starts in 20 seconds! Click here to teleport to Garry and prepare!|
|Event starting|----------------------------------------------------- MITHRIL GOURMAND STARTED! Active Mining Event Don Expresso wants some Tasty Mithril from the <location>. Bring 100-200 Tasty Mithril to him at the Dwarven Village! Bring 10 personally and you'll receive extra rewards. Go to <location> (or talk to Garry) -----------------------------------------------------|
|Upon reaching 100–200x [[Tasty Mithril]] donated.|Don Expresso is leaving in 1 minute! Make sure to feed him everything you have before he leaves!|
|20 second left without reaching a milestone|Don Expresso is leaving in 20 seconds! Make sure to feed him everything you have before he leaves! [Teleport to Don Expresso]|
|Event ending Participating|----------------------------------------------------- MITHRIL GOURMAND ENDED! Active Mining Event -----------------------------------------------------|
|Event ending Not participating|----------------------------------------------------- MITHRIL GOURMAND ENDED! Active Mining Event You didn't participate... -----------------------------------------------------|
## Tips
- Players can use the [[Reforging|Royal]] reforge to increase their gain of ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] from this and other [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]].
- It is recommended to wait until the last minute to cash in [[Tasty Mithril|Tasty Mithril clumps]] as the individual ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] reward scale with the amount of [[Tasty Mithril]] donated. This allows for all participants to fully benefit from the event.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|Aug 20, 2024|Each Tasty Mithril turned in to Don Expresso grants 50 Mithril Powder.|
|Aug 20, 2024|If the lobby satisfies Don Expresso, everyone gets doubled Mithril Powder rewards.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Raised the participation bonus from 2,000 to 10,000.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Removed the cap on donated Tasty Mithril.|

# Raffle of the Century
Aliases: Century, Century Celebration, Century Raffle, The Big One, Year 500
Categories: Templates with deprecated argument names, Confirmations needed, Article stubs, Events
Year 600 Raffle of the Century
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
[Raffle of the Century]
Category: Event
The Raffle of the Century is an [[Events|Event]] that happens every 100 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Years]].
## Mechanics
The Raffle of the Century has 3 tiers of raffles known as the [[Raffle of the Century]] Speed Raffle, [[Raffle of the Century]] Daily Raffle and [[Raffle of the Century]] The Big One. The [[Raffle of the Century]] Speed Raffle rolls every 2 hours, the [[Raffle of the Century]] Daily Raffle every 24 hours, and [[Raffle of the Century]] The Big One at the end of the event.
In order to participate in the Raffle of the Century, the player must speak to [[Rezzus]] in the [[Village]] to be assigned to one of the following teams: BLUE, YELLOW, GREEN, RED or PINK. Upon joining a team, the player gains 3 Raffle Tickets and a corresponding team [[SkyBlock Levels|Emblem]] is applied for the remainder of the event and cannot be removed.
After a player is assigned to a team, they can then either speak to the [[Incredible Raffle Box]], open the [[Incredible Raffle Box]] in the [[SkyBlock Menu]], or use /century or /centuryrafflebox to see all the rewards, tasks, and current raffles.
## Earning Tickets
To enter a raffle, the player must earn Raffle Tickets. Raffle Tickets can be earned through various methods.
### Being Online
The player earns 1 Raffle Ticket for every 15 minutes of being online, totaling 96 Raffle Tickets per day.
### Raffle Tasks
Every 2 hours, players receive an identical list of 21 total tasks, with 7 of each (easy, medium and hard) difficulty. Each completed task grants 1 [[Cake Slice]] and 1 Raffle Ticket.
Possible Tasks:
|Easy|Medium|Hard|
|-|-|-|

|Catch a Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]).|Catch a [[Mithril Grubber]].|Catch a [[Carrot King]].|

## Rewards
Each raffle draws a certain amount of items from 3 reward tiers, being major, medium, and minor. The odds distribution of items within a reward tier is unknown, as that information was not allowed to be shared despite request.

### Consolation Rewards
If the player does not get a major, medium, or minor reward, they instead gain a consolation reward.
|Raffle Type|Consolation Reward|
|-|-|
|[[Raffle of the Century]] Speed Raffle|Random [[Cake Slice]] for every 5 tickets entered (max 10 tickets).|
|[[Raffle of the Century]] Daily Raffle|Random [[Cake Slice]] for every 5 tickets entered (max 25 tickets).|
|[[Raffle of the Century]] The Big One|Random Century Cake ([[Century Cakes]]) if entered with at least 50 tickets.; Granted even if the player wins in the raffle.|
### Milestone
Earning Raffle Tickets progresses milestones. Reaching milestone tiers grants rewards.
|Milestone|Tickets|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|I|1|10,000 [[Coins]] 1 Random Slice of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|II|10|⛂ Century Celebrant Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) 2 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|III|25|[[Ancient Turtle Skin]] 3 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|IV|50|[[Talisman of the Century]] 4 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|V|100|⛂ Century Partygoer Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) 5 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|VI|200|[[Gift Bag of the Century]] 6 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
|VII|400|[[Century the Fish]] 7 Random Slices of Century Cake ([[Cake Slice]])|
### Miscellaneous
During the event, players can talk to [[Simon]] to receive a [[Century Cakes|Random Century Cake]].
Once the Raffle of the Century ends, each player can claim a [[Cake Slice of the Century]] as a [[Mementos|Memento]]. Only 1 [[Cake Slice of the Century]] can be claimed per account.
## Previous Events
During Year 400 tickets could be obtained by three different methods:
#### Being Online
The player earns 1 Raffle Ticket every 30 minutes being online, up to 12 hours, totaling 24 Raffle Tickets per day.
#### Daily Quests
Completing Daily Quests reward 1 Raffle Ticket for every completed Quest, there are 63 total Quests split into 21 easy, medium and hard quests.

Random [[Cake Slice]] if entered with at least 5 tickets.

Milestones and amount of raffle rewards were the same as Year 500.
During Year 300 tickets could be obtained by two different methods:
- 1 Ticket every 20 minutes while being online
- 1 Ticket every 20 minutes when gaining Skill XP or [[Bits]] with 1% chance.

If player lost in the raffle, they would get a Consolation reward.
### Consolation Rewards
#### [[Raffle of the Century]] Speed Raffle
25,000 [[Coins]]
#### [[Raffle of the Century]] Daily Raffle
The player will receive up to 5 rewards based on the amount of tickets they obtained:
- 1+ Tickets: 1 Reward
- 5+ Tickets: 2 Rewards
- 15+ Tickets: 3 Rewards
- 25+ Tickets: 4 Rewards
- 50+ Tickets: 5 Rewards
The player can receive the following rewards:
- [[Coins]]: Tickets×1000
- ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]: Tickets×1000
- ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]:Tickets×1000
- [[Combat]], [[Fishing]], [[Foraging]] or [[Mining]] XP: Tickets×500
- [[White Gift]]: max⁡(Tickets10,1)
Tickets is the amount of Tickets the player entered, capped at 100.
#### [[Raffle of the Century]] The Big One
The player will receive up to 5 rewards based on their Raffle Milestone.
The player can receive the following rewards:
- [[Coins]]: Tickets×2000
- ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]: Tickets×2000
- ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]]:Tickets×2000
- [[Combat]], [[Fishing]], [[Foraging]] or [[Mining]] XP: Tickets×1000
- [[White Gift]]: max⁡(Tickets5,1)
Tickets is the amount of Tickets the player entered, capped at 100.
### Milestones
Earning Raffle Tickets are tracked in a milestone that gives rewards.

|-|-|-|
|I|5|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|II|15|⛂ Century Celebrant Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|III|50|[[Ancient Turtle Skin]]|
|IV|100|[[Talisman of the Century]]|
|V|150|⛂ Century Partygoer Emblem ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Playtime Raffle Ticket|PLAYTIME! You gained +1 Raffle Ticket!|
|Raffle Task completion|RAFFLE TASK! You completed the <task name> raffle task and earned +1 Raffle Ticket and a slice of cake!|
|Raffle Tasks refreshed|Your Raffle Tasks have refreshed! Click HERE to view your new ones!|
|New Milestone Reached|MILESTONE! You reached Raffle Milestone #! [CLICK TO VIEW]|
|Claiming the Milestone|You claimed the Raffle Milestone # reward, gg!|
|Raffle is drawn|RAFFLE! <raffle type> # is being drawn! A total of # tickets were entered, with <team name> having the most entries!|
|Raffle is drawn|RAFFLE! <raffle type> # has been drawn! Check the Raffle Box or click HERE to see if you won!|
|Claiming a prize|Grabbing your prize...|
|Claiming a prize|Checking escrow for recent transaction...|
|After claiming a prize|You claimed your prize from <raffle type> #!|
|After claiming a prize|RAFFLE! <player> won <item name> in <raffle type> #!|
|After claiming a consolation prize|You claimed your compensation prize from <raffle type> #!|
|Entering the game; during Year 500|CENTURY CELEBRATION BUFFS +50% Skill XP +50% Dungeon XP & Class XP +50% Slayer XP +50✯ Magic Find +50❃ Tracking|
|Trying to open the [[Incredible Raffle Box]]; through commands on a Ⓑ Bingo ([[Bingo]]) Profile|Sorry, you cannot open the raffle box on a profile of this game mode.|
## Notes
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: More interactions with XP boosts from this event and other sources
See more stubs.
- Aatrox's bonus to Slayer XP is multiplicative with the one from [[Raffle of the Century|Century Celebration]].
## Trivia
- According to [[Simon]], the Year 500 Raffle was supplied heavily by [[Sirius]].
- The button for the [[Incredible Raffle Box]] in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] does not appear when on a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] Profile. Instead it will be an empty slot, the slot is not even filled with a blank glass pane.
- The "Eat a slice of Century Cake" raffle task can be completed in multiple ways, unlike other tasks.
  - By either eating a [[Cake Slice|Century Cake Slice]] fed to you by another player.
  - By right clicking on a [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] on a [[Private Island]].

# Stranded/Trophy Fish
Categories: Templates with deprecated argument names
|Item|Requirement|Chance|Changed (Whether or not the method to obtain the Trophy Fish is different on Stranded than it is in other gamemodes)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Blobfish|No requirement.|15%|✕|
|Flyfish|The bobber must be at least 8 blocks below the player.|8%|✕|
|Golden Fish|Fishing for 15+ minutes. See Trophy Fishes § Golden Fish ([[Trophy Fish]]).||✕|
|Gusher|The biome must be changed with a Nether Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]).|20%|✔|
|Karate Fish|The player must punch the air and/or blocks at least 20 times after casting.|1%|✔|
|Lavahorse|No requirement.|4%|✕|
|Mana Ray|The player must have at least 1200 [[Mana]].|4%|✕|
|Moldfin|There must be at least 8 Mycelium ([[Stranded]]) blocks within 5 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Skeleton Fish|There must be a Skeleton ([[Stranded]]) within 8 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Slugfish|At least 20 seconds must pass between casting the rod and reeling it in.|10%|✕|
|Soul Fish|There must be at least 8 [[Soul Sand]] blocks within 5 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Steaming-Hot Flounder|It must be raining, which can be controlled with a Weather Stick ([[Weather Wand]]).|20%|✔|
|Sulphur Skitter|There must be at least 8 Sulphur ([[Stranded]]) in the player's inventory.|30%|✔|
|Vanille|The player must use a clean [[Starter Lava Rod]]. Rod swapping makes this easier.|3%|✕|
|Volcanic Stonefish|There must be a [[Hard Stone Minion]] within 8 blocks of the bobber.|3%|✔|
|Obfuscated 1|The player must use [[Corrupted Bait]].|25%|✕|
|Obfuscated 2|The player must use Obfuscated 1 as bait.|20%|✕|
|Obfuscated 3|The player must use Obfuscated 2 as bait.|10%|✕|
Note: Obfuscated 1 Trophy Fish can also be obtained by killing [[Corrupted Mobs|Corrupted]] Sea Creatures, so using a [[Totem of Corruption]] on Sea Creatures to get a lot of Obfuscated 1 will help speed up the process when trying to get Obfuscated 2 and Obfuscated 3. Getting Obfuscated 1 from Corrupted Sea Creatures doesn't count for [[Odger]]'s quest, so this method should only be used once all tiers of Obfuscated 1 have been obtained using [[Corrupted Bait]].
Linked from: [[Stranded]]

# Draconic Altar
Aliases: Dragon Altar, Dragon Room, Dragon altar, Dragon room, Dragonic Altar, Dragonic altar, Sacrifice
Categories: The End
[Draconic Altar]
The Draconic Altar is a feature in the [[Void Slate]] that allows players to sacrifice certain items in order to receive [[Essence|Dragon Essence]], [[Dragon Fragment|Dragon Fragments]] and [[Enchanted Ender Pearl|Enchanted Ender Pearls]], and other items.
## Mechanics
Certain items can be sacrificed to the Draconic Altar by right clicking on it with the item in hand. The player will always receive an amount of [[Dragon Essence]]. There is also a 45% chance for bonus items to be rolled, which can be increased by up to 10% with the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Endermite Pet]]'s Sacrificer ability.

# Black Holes
Aliases: Black Hole, Blackhole, Pocket Black Hole, Pocket Black Hole (disambiguation), Pocket Black Holes
Categories: Tools
[Black Holes]
Black Holes are a type of [[Hunting]] [[Deployables|Deployable]]. They are used in [[Hunting]] to hunt creatures at low [[Health]] to obtain their respective [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].
## Black Holes
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Small Pocket Black Hole]]|[[Small Pocket Black Hole]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|[[Albert]]|
|[[Medium Pocket Black Hole]]|[[Medium Pocket Black Hole]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Albert]]|
|[[Large Pocket Black Hole]]|[[Large Pocket Black Hole]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Crafting]] - Ruby Veilshroom VIII (8) ([[Ruby Veilshroom]])|
## Usage
A Black Hole can be placed down to draw in nearby [[Mob|Mobs]]. Having lower [[Health]] causes the Black Hole to pull it in stronger. Once an eligible [[Mob]] has less than 10% [[Health]] and is within range, the Black Hole can catch it to gain its [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]]. Black Holes despawn after 180 seconds and can have the timer reset by catching a [[Mob]].
Additionally, the [[Medium Pocket Black Hole|Medium]] and [[Large Pocket Black Hole|Large Pocket Black Holes]] grant +2.5 and +5 [[Hunting Fortune]] for Combat Shards respectively.
## Tips
- Black Holes are best paired with [[Huntaxes]], due to them preventing fatal blows.
- When capturing a mob, the Black Hole will play an animation lasting 2 seconds for each level of [[Rarity]] of the shard being captured. For example, an [[Rarity|EPIC]] shard would take 8 seconds to capture. The Black Hole cannot capture another mob during this animation and shortly after it ends.
  - This animation can be shortened in the following ways:
    - [[Attributes|Hunter's Pressure]] attribute by up to 50% (60% with [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter]], 65% with [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter]] and [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]])
    - [[Accretion Talisman]], [[Accretion Ring]] and [[Accretion Artifact]] by 5%, 10% and 15% respectivelly.
    - [[Desert Temple]] Benefactor bonus by 25%
  - The delay after finishing a capture can be skipped by immediately placing down the Black Hole again.
- Due to the [[Attributes|Hunter’s Grasp]] attribute increasing the [[Hunting Fortune]] of Black Holes by up to 13%, using Black Holes can yield more [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] than other methods of hunting the same mob.
## Trivia
- Black Holes cannot be placed in the [[Catacombs]]. Attempting to do so will issue the following message: Pocket Black Holes cannot be placed inside The Catacombs!
  - As a result, the only way to obtain [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] from Catacombs mobs is through Charming. The [[Charm Chance]] stat can be increased through the use of [[Salts]], the [[Attributes|Charmed]] [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], or by leveling the [[Hunting]] Skill.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added [[Small Pocket Black Hole]].|
|Jun 5, 2025|Added [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added [[Large Pocket Black Hole]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added [[Large Pocket Black Hole]].|

# Chisels
Categories: Tools
[Chisels]
Chisels are used at the [[Fossil Excavator]] in the [[Fossil Research Center]], alongside [[Suspicious Scrap]] to use the [[Fossil Excavator]].
## Usage
Each Chisel has a set amount of Charges, which increase with rarity, giving you more chances to find treasure in the [[Fossil Excavator]].
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Charges|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Chisel]]|[[Chisel]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|16|
|[[Reinforced Chisel]]|[[Reinforced Chisel]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|18|
|[[Glacite-Plated Chisel]]|[[Glacite-Plated Chisel]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|20|
|[[Perfect Chisel]]|[[Perfect Chisel]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|22|
## Obtaining
The [[Rarity|COMMON]] Chisel is given to the [[Player]] the first time they talk to [[Dr. Stone]]; alternatively it can be bought from [[Researcher Beryl]] at the [[Fossil Research Center]] with 5 [[Fossil Essence]]. The Chisel can then be upgraded by forging in [[The Forge]]. Each upgrade has a [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier requirement.
|Chisel|Recipe|HotM Requirement|
|-|-|-|
|[[Reinforced Chisel]]|1 [[Reinforced Chisel]] 1 [[Chisel]] 2 [[Refined Tungsten]] 2 [[Refined Umber]] 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]]|[[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 8|
|[[Glacite-Plated Chisel]]|1 [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]] 1 [[Reinforced Chisel]] 8 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]]|[[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 9|
|[[Perfect Chisel]]|1 [[Perfect Chisel]] 1 [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]] 1 [[Perfect Plate]] 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]]|[[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 10|
## Gemstone Slots
Chisels gain an extra [[Gemstone Slot|Chisel Gemstone Slot]] after each upgrade. For example, the initial [[Chisel]] has no gemstone slots, but the [[Perfect Chisel]] has 3.
Each gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel: Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:
|Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Name|Bonus|Value by gemstone tier Rough|Value by gemstone tier Flawed|Value by gemstone tier Fine|Value by gemstone tier Flawless|Value by gemstone tier Perfect|

# Drill Parts
Aliases: Drill Engine, Drill Engine (disambiguation), Drill Engines, Drill Part, Drill Upgrades, Drill upgrades, Fuel Tanks, Omelette, Omelette (disambiguation), Omlette, Upgrade Module, Upgrade Modules, Upgrade module
Categories: Tools, Mining
[Drill Parts]
Drill Parts are a core part of [[Mining]]. They can be applied to [[Drills]] to grant various bonuses.
## Overview
[[Drills]] can have 3 Drill Parts applied at once. They can be applied at [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] in the [[Forge Basin]] for various perks. There are 3 types of Drill Parts:
- Fuel Tank increases the fuel of the [[Drills|Drill]] and lowers ability cooldown.
- Drill Engine grant stat bonuses.
- Upgrade Module grant [[Drills]] miscellaneous special abilities.
Only one type of Drill Part may be applied to a [[Drills]] at once. If the player wants to apply a different Drill Part, they need to remove the previous one first.

# Drills
Aliases: Drill, Fuel Tank, Fuel tank
Categories: Tools, Mining
[Drills]
Drills are tools used in the [[Mining]] skill to obtain ore and stone, similar to [[Pickaxes]].
## Mechanics
Drills are mining tools for rock-based materials. They can use the same [[Enchantments]] and [[Reforging|Reforges]] as pickaxes do. They also have a [[Breaking Power]] just like pickaxes.
Drills only work on islands that utilize the custom mining system that uses [[Mining Speed]] instead of the vanilla mining system: this includes the [[Gold Mine]], the [[Deep Caverns]], the [[Dwarven Mines]], the [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]], [[The End]], the [[Crimson Isle]], and in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
Drills require [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] to function. Most blocks cost 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] to mine, [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] Gemstones cost 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] to mine, whereas [[Glacite]], [[Tungsten]] and [[Umber]] cost 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] to mine.
The remaining Drill Fuel can be seen on the action bar.
## Upgrading
On upgrading a Drill to a higher tier, such as forging a [[Mithril Drill SX-R226]] into a [[Mithril Drill SX-R326]], it will keep all its Drill Parts and extra stats, including [[Enchantments]], [[Reforging|Reforges]], and used [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulators]]. It should be noted that the [[Titanium Drill DR-X355]] is not an upgrade of the [[Mithril Drill SX-R326]], and is instead crafted purely from raw materials.
## Drill Parts
Drill Parts are additional upgrades for Drills. These can be combined to a drill using a [[Drill Anvil]]. There are three available categories of Drill Parts, being Fuel Tanks, Drill Engines, and Upgrade Modules.

## Tips
- The [[Mithril Drill SX-R326]] is generally not recommended, since it has low [[Breaking Power]], and is very expensive for its stats: however, it is excellent for ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] gain.
- If player wishes to progress via [[Crystal Hollows]], they should use the Gemstone Drill line since they're cheap and grant very useful [[Pristine]] as well as other bonuses when mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
  - This is true until the player can afford [[Titanium Drill DR-X655]] which is universal and the second best mining tool, due to it having a slot for [[Gemstone|Topaz]] gemstone, unlike its predecessors.
- On the other side, if the player wishes to progress through [[Dwarven Mines]], the Titanium Drills become a good alternative to Mithril gear.
## Notes
- Extra blocks mined with the Efficient Miner HotM perk do not consume extra [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].
## Trivia
- On August 23, 2022 all Drills would give 50-550 (depending on [[Drills|Drill Engine]]) of each stat due to a bug, this was fixed minutes later.

# Fishing Nets
Aliases: Fishing Net, Net
Categories: Tools
[Fishing Nets]
Fishing Nets are a type of [[Hunting]] tool. They can be used in [[Hunting|Water Hunts]] to catch [[Attributes|Water Attribute Shards]]. Different Fishing Nets have different amounts of [[Pull]], which affects what creatures you can catch and how much damage you deal.
## Fishing Nets
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|Base Pull|Throw Distance|Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Basic Fishing Net]]|[[Basic Fishing Net]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Sea Lumies I (1) ([[Sea Lumies]]); 1 given for free by [[Jaeger]]|+5 [[Pull]]|8 blocks||
|[[Medium Fishing Net]]|[[Medium Fishing Net]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Sea Lumies V (5) ([[Sea Lumies]])|+25 [[Pull]]|12 blocks|+2.5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Water Shards|
|[[Turbo Fishing Net]]|[[Turbo Fishing Net]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Sea Lumies IX (9) ([[Sea Lumies]])|+50 [[Pull]]|16 blocks|+5 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Water Shards|
|[[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|[[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Sea Lumies X (10) ([[Sea Lumies]])|+100 [[Pull]]|20 blocks|+10 [[Hunting Fortune]] on Water Shards|
## Usage
Fishing Nets can be used to catch [[Attributes|Water Attribute Shards]].
- [[Cod]], [[Verdant]], [[Birries]], [[Sepialot]], [[Goldolot]], [[Coralot]], [[Lotusfish]], [[Azure]], [[Solar]], [[Ember]], [[Timil]], [[Salmon]], [[Joydive|Joydives]], [[Lumisquid|Lumisquids]], and [[Shellwise|Shellwises]] can be caught with any type of Fishing Net.
- [[Flipflopper|Flipfloppers]], and [[Seashine|Seashines]] require a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better.
- [[Spike|Spikes]], and [[Water Snake|Water Snakes]] require a [[Turbo Fishing Net]] or better.
Fishing Nets can only be used on the islands of [[Galatea]] or the [[Lotus Atoll]]. Attempting to use a Fishing Net outside of these regions displays the following message:
You need to be on Galatea or Lotus Atoll to use this item!

# Fishing Rods
Aliases: Fishing Rod, Fishing Weapons, Fishing rod, Lava Rod, Lava Rods, Rod, Rods
Categories: Tools, Fishing
[Fishing Rods]
Fishing Rods are a core aspect of [[Fishing]] as well as increasing fishing related [[Collections]].
Fishing rods deal double damage to [[Sea Creatures]].
## Modifiers
### Reforges
When applying basic Reforges, Fishing Rods share the same [[Reforging|Reforges]] that [[Swords]] have.
Additionally, Fishing Rods can have certain [[Reforge Stones/Fishing Rod|Reforge Stones]] applied which cannot be applied to other tools/weapons.
### Enchantments
Fishing Rods can be enchanted with various Enchantments to grant various perks and buffs to the Fishing Rod, some enchantments have a higher tier that requires a special item to be used in an anvil in order to obtain, such as [[Gold Bottle Cap]] which must be used on a Fishing Rod with [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VI]] to upgrade it to [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VII]].
## Rod Parts
Fishing rods can equip 3 [[Rod Parts]] at [[Roddy]] for varying perks, a Hook which grants chance to catch certain things, a Line which grants stat bonuses everywhere and a Sinker which gives special abilities.

### Fishing Weapons
Fishing Weapons was a class of weapons, featuring only [[Phantom Rod]] and [[Shredder]], both of which were removed in 0.22, and with them the entire class.
They could have been enchanted with both [[Swords|Sword]] and Fishing Rod [[Enchantments]].
## Trivia
- Despite not having the same properties as a fishing rod, the [[Nearly Coherent doR gnihsiF]] has "fishing rod" backwards in its name.
- In the Spooky Fishing update, a new type of fishing rod was introduced. Fishing Weapons were fishing rods that are meant for combat as well as fishing, and can have sword [[Enchantments]] in addition to fishing rod Enchantments. There were two Fishing Weapons: the [[Shredder]] and the [[Phantom Rod]].
  - Even though they have the appearance of fishing rods, the [[Zombie Commander Whip]] and the [[Soul Whip]] are not Fishing Weapons, and cannot have fishing rod Enchantments.
  - Fishing Weapons have since been removed alongside most Fishing Rods in The Backwater Bayou update.

# Gauntlet
Categories: Tools
[Gauntlet]
Gauntlets are items that function as both a [[Swords|Sword]] and a [[Tools|Mining Tool]]. Currently, the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] is the only Gauntlet in-game.
## Mechanics
Gauntlets serve as both [[Swords]] and [[Tools|Mining Tools]]: as such, modifiers designed for items in both categories can be applied to a Gauntlet (e.g. a Gauntlet can have both [[Sharpness]] and [[Efficiency]]).

# Huntaxes
Aliases: Hunt Axe, Hunt axe, Huntaxe, Hunting Axe
Categories: Tools
[Huntaxes]
Huntaxes are a type of [[Hunting]] tool. They are used to [[Hunting|hunt]] creatures by lowering their [[Health]] to below 10%, allowing them to be captured with [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Holes]]. Huntaxes deal little damage, but can hold weapons to absorb some of their stats. They also prevent the player from killing mobs that they are hunting.
## Huntaxe
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|
|[[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Vinesap I (1) ([[Vinesap]])|
|[[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Vinesap III (3) ([[Vinesap]])|
|[[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Vinesap V (5) ([[Vinesap]])|
|[[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Vinesap VII (7) ([[Vinesap]])|
|[[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Vinesap IX (9) ([[Vinesap]])|
## Usage
A Huntaxe can be used to [[Hunting|Hunt]] creatures with an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]]. They have two abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Huntaxe to absorb up to 25% of the basic [[Combat]] stats ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], etc) of any melee weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of a Huntaxe up to 50% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy.
## Tips
- You are not required to use Huntaxes to hunt mobs with Pocket Black Holes.
  - This is important when hunting mobs with high [[Health]] that the Huntaxes cannot reasonably deal with. A notable example are [[Ghost]].
- Each tier of Huntaxes allows to bring mobs HP to the 10% [[Health]] threshold in 6/5/4/3/2 hits.
- The [[Attributes|Hunter's Fang]] attributes grants up to +10% of the stored weapon's stats to the Huntaxes.
  - [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter]], paired with [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] can increase this amount to 13%, up to a maximum of 38% on [[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum]].
## Notes
- While holding a Huntaxe, all damage dealt by the player becomes non lethal.
  - Notably, this allow the usage of certain passive damage sources, such as the Blazing Aura ability of the [[Blaze Armor]] and [[Frozen Blaze Armor]], or the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wither Skeleton Pet]]'s Death's Touch perk.
- When putting a weapon to store in the Huntaxes with its ability Absorptio, only [[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]] and [[Crit Damage]] are copied. Enchantments (other than [[Critical]] and [[Chimera]]) will have no impact on the performance of the Huntaxes.
  - To this end, the [[Tormentor]] is commonly agreed upon to be the best-in-slot item to store in the Huntaxes.
  - If the player has multiple Huntaxes, all of them will have the same item.
- Players can use [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]], [[Reforging|Rugged]] and [[Woodsplitter]] to make their Huntaxes stronger.
  - [[Jade Dragon Pet]]'s Forest Power perk and [[Rosemary]]'s Axed perk also affect Huntaxes.
## Trivia
- Before the perk being reworked, [[Golden Dragon Pet]]'s Gold Power worked on Huntaxes.
- Despite the ability claiming to only be affected by base stats, Absorptio copies stats from all sources. Because of this, the same item can be affected by 2 different reforges.

# Sprayonators
Aliases: Nozzle
Categories: Tools
[Sprayonators]
Sprayonators are a type of [[Farming]] tool. They are used to spray plots while on [[The Garden]].
## Sprayonators
### List
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Cost|Cost (cumulative)|Duration|Nozzles|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sprayonator]]|[[Sprayonator]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25 [[Copper]]|25 [[Copper]]|30 minutes|Basic Nozzle|
|[[Juicy Sprayonator]]|[[Juicy Sprayonator]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1,000 [[Copper]] 1x [[Sprayonator]] 1x [[Juicy Nozzle]]|1,025 [[Copper]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Melon Slice]]|45 minutes|Basic Nozzle, [[Juicy Nozzle]]|
|[[Salty Sprayonator]]|[[Salty Sprayonator]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Copper]] 1x [[Juicy Sprayonator]] 1x [[Salty Nozzle]]|11,025 [[Copper]] 30,720x [[Melon Slice]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Salted Sunflower Seeds]]|60 minutes|Basic Nozzle, [[Juicy Nozzle]], [[Salty Nozzle]]|
## Usage
All Sprayonators require [[The Garden|Garden V (5)]] to use.
### Attracting Pests
The Sprayonators can be used with the appropriate materials to attract certain [[Pests]] to plots. When a spray is active, [[Pests]] are guaranteed to spawn in the plot the player is farming in, or one of the four directly adjacent plots.
The materials below can be used to attract specific pests:
|Material|Attracted Pests|
|-|-|
|[[Compost]]|[[Earthworm]], [[Mosquito]]|
|[[Plant Matter]]|[[Locust]], [[Slug]]|
|[[Dung]]|[[Beetle]], [[Fly]]|
|[[Honey Jar]]|[[Moth]], [[Cricket]]|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|[[Rat]], [[Mite]]|
|[[Jelly]]|[[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], [[Firefly]]|
The weights of the targeted [[Pests]] are increased by +1,000, on top of the base 100 weight.
### Nozzles
Depending on the Nozzle currently selected, which can be changed in the Sprayonator interface, using the Sprayonator will consume varying amounts of materials and provide different buffs:
- Basic Nozzle: Consumes 1 of the material. Grants +25 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
- [[Juicy Nozzle]]: Consumes 3 of the material. Grants +50 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 2x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
- [[Salty Nozzle]]: Consumes 5 of the material. Grants +75 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 3x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
### Removing Sprayonator Effects
[[Portable Washer]] can be used to remove all active Sprayonator effects while in [[The Garden]]. Pasting a plot preset onto a sprayed plot will also remove the active Sprayonator effect in that plot.
## Trivia
- The Slug Pet's Repugnant Aroma perk grants up to +100 [[Farming Fortune]] in Sprayed Plots. This is affected by the [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]], resulting in the ability granting up to +200 [[Farming Fortune]] overall.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Added Sprayonator.|
Linked from: [[Bucket (disambiguation)]], [[Pest Vinyls]]

# Tools
Aliases: Mining Tool, Mining Tools, Tool
Categories: Tools, Mechanics
Tools are items that when held can be used to perform certain actions or gather materials.
## Normal Tools
Normal Tools can be used to get their corresponding materials faster. In the case of pickaxes, some blocks can only be obtained when the pickaxe has sufficient [[Breaking Power]].
|Tool|Usage|
|-|-|
|[[Pickaxes]]; [[Drills]]|[[Mining]] different Ores, Blocks, Dwarven Metals, and Gemstones|
|[[Axes]]|Chopping down trees and obtaining wood|
|[[Shovels]]|Mining certain blocks|
|[[Farming Tools]]|Grant stats when Farming.|
|[[Fishing Rods]]|[[Fishing]] up items and [[Sea Creatures]]|
|[[Gauntlet]]|Combine the uses of Pickaxes, Drills and of a Weapon ([[Weapons]]). [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] is the sole case of this.|
|Fishing Weapons ([[Fishing Rods]])|Combines the uses of a Fishing Rod and of a Weapon ([[Weapons]]). [[Phantom Rod]] and [[Shredder]] were the sole cases of this.|
## Misc Tools
These tools have unique capabilities.
|Icon|Tool|Usage|
|-|-|-|
|[[Grappling Hook]]|[[Grappling Hook]]|Traveling through the air at high speeds|
|[[Biome Stick]]|[[Biome Stick]]|Changing the biome of the [[Private Island]]|
|[[Spray Can]]|[[Spray Can]]|Used to place pre-made map art on the Private Island|
|[[Maddox Batphone]]|[[Maddox Batphone]]|Lets the player talk to Maddox ([[Maddox the Slayer]])|
|[[Royal Pigeon]]|[[Royal Pigeon]]|Lets the player talk to the [[King]]|
||[[Support Items]]|Various items that aid in [[Combat]]|
## Utility Items
Although they are not exactly tools, these items have similar effects to the Misc Tools. They can only be placed on the [[Private Island]].
|Icon|Tool|Usage|

|[[Night Saver]]|[[Night Saver]]|Makes it always nighttime|

|[[Repelling Candle]]|[[Repelling Candle]]|Prevents mob spawns|

# Vacuums
Aliases: Vaccum, Vaccums, Vacuum
Categories: Tools
[Vacuums]
Vacuums are a type of [[Farming]] tool. They are used to vacuum [[Pests]] while on [[The Garden]].
## Vacuums
### List
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Cost|Cost (cumulative)|Damage|Farming Fortune|Range|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[SkyMart Vacuum]]|[[SkyMart Vacuum]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|+100 [[Damage]]|+5 [[Farming Fortune]]|5 blocks|
|[[SkyMart Turbo Vacuum]]|[[SkyMart Turbo Vacuum]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|200 [[Copper]] 1x [[SkyMart Vacuum]]|5,000 [[Coins]] 200 [[Copper]]|+150 [[Damage]]|+10 [[Farming Fortune]]|7.5 blocks|
|[[SkyMart Hyper Vacuum]]|[[SkyMart Hyper Vacuum]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|500 [[Copper]] 1x [[SkyMart Turbo Vacuum]]|5,000 [[Coins]] 700 [[Copper]]|+200 [[Damage]]|+15 [[Farming Fortune]]|10 blocks|
|[[InfiniVacuum™]]|[[InfiniVacuum™]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000 [[Copper]] [[SkyMart Hyper Vacuum]]|5,000 [[Coins]] 1,700 [[Copper]]|+300 [[Damage]]|+20 [[Farming Fortune]]|12.5 blocks|
|[[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]]|[[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|2,500 [[Copper]] [[Chirping Stereo]] 1x [[InfiniVacuum™]]|5,000 [[Coins]] 4,200 [[Copper]] 1x [[Chirping Stereo]]|+400 [[Damage]]|+25 [[Farming Fortune]]|15 blocks|
## Usage
Vacuums can vacuum [[Pests]] on [[The Garden]] or in [[Ant's Cave]]. They have up to 3 abilities depending on their [[Rarity]]:
- Vacuum deals [[Damage]] to nearby Pests and pulls them towards the player. Killing a Pest by using a Vacuum on [[The Garden]] grants 1 [[Pests (Currency)|Pest]].
- Pest Tracker creates a line of particles in the direction of the nearest Pest while on [[The Garden]].
- Stereo Harmony allows the player to play [[Pest Vinyls]], which will increase the chances of specific Pests spawning.
## Upgrades
Multiple upgrades can be applied to vacuums.
- [[Bookworm's Favorite Book]] - Can be applied to a vacuum up to 5 times, granting +20 [[Damage]] per book.
- [[Farming for Dummies]] - Can be applied to a vacuum up to 5 times, granting +1 [[Farming Fortune]] per book.
- [[Reforging|Reforges]]:
  - [[Beady Eyes]]
  - [[Clipped Wings]]
- [[Gemstone|Gemstones]]
- [[Bug Blender]]
## Trivia
- Clicking other players or a non-[[Pests|pest]] mob with a vacuum will send the message Vacuums don't work on these creatures!

|[[HydroCan™ Ultra 3000]]|[[HydroCan™ Ultra 3000]]|10,000|250/s|2x2|
|[[AquaMaster™ X]]|[[AquaMaster™ X]]|15,000|300/s|2x2|
|[[AquaMaster™ Hydromax]]|[[AquaMaster™ Hydromax]]|20,000|350/s|3x3|
## Usage
Watering Cans can retain water to be used on [[Crops (disambiguation)|crops]] in the [[Greenhouse]]. They have up to 3 abilities depending on their [[Rarity]]:
- Hydrate dispenses between 150 and 350 water per second.
- Fill refills the Watering Can to its maximum capacity, between 3k and 20k water. Using Hydrate whilst refilling a Watering Can does not interrupt its refill, nor does it offset the amount of water gained as it will always return to its maximum capacity.
- Aquaburst allows the player to water multiple crops at one, in a 2x2 or 3x3 area.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Watering Cans.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Watering Cans.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Watering Cans now use durability bar to show amount of water left.|
Linked from: [[AquaMaster™ Hydromax]], [[AquaMaster™ X]], [[Greenhouse]], [[HydroCan™ 1000]], [[HydroCan™ Turbo 2000]], [[HydroCan™ Ultra 3000]], [[Item Types]], [[Sam]], [[Water (disambiguation)]]
